```
def multiplicative_flow(a0, time_limit, seed, tau_start=0.2, tau_min=0.001, lr_start=1.0, max_iters=None):
   a = norm(a0); loga=log(a)
   best=a; bestM=conv(a).max()
   rng...
   stall=0; adaptive lr=lr_start
   for t in while time:
       tau = tau_start * exp(-t/decay) etc. maybe anneal to tau_min
       b,G,dot,bmax=conv_grad(a,tau)
       soft=...
       if bmax<bestM: best...
       delta=G - dot
       # optionally only modify if delta variance > eps
       # line search on exact M or soft
       full=None
       for factor in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25]:
           a_trial=softmax(loga - factor*delta)
           M_trial=conv(a_trial).max()
           # maybe use soft if conv already? we have conv for trial only M, no G. For line search exact M okay.
       pick best score
       if best_trial M < current exact M? update loga and a; else if not improved, reduce lr or perturb.
```

def fourier_search(n, K, time_limit, seed, tau_rel=1e-3, ...):
    basis = make_fourier_basis(n,K)
    best over multiple random starts:
      c = rng.normal(0,scale)
      for it:
         a = softmax(basis@c)
         b,G,dot,soft = conv_grad(a,tau_rel)
         if bmax exact < best ...
         theta = a*(G-dot)
         gradc = basis.T @ theta
         Adam update c -= lr*grad_hat
         optional line search on soft? For small dim, line search possible: evaluate objective at c and c - factor*adam_step? Need a and conv. Option 1: Exponential flow with adaptive step and random restarts.
Pseudo:
```
def flow_optimize(a0, time_limit, seed, tau_rel_start=0.2, tau_rel_end=0.002, lr_base=2.0):
  n=len(a)
  a=normalize positive
  loga=np.log(a+a.min?)
  best=a; bestM=conv_max(a); bestscore=2n*bestM
  lr=lr_base
  rng...
  stall=0; no_improve=0; phase...
  t=0
  while time < time_limit:
    tau = tau_start * exp(-t/decay) clipped
    b,G,dot,bmax=conv_grad(a,tau)
    if bmax < bestM: update best
    delta = G - dot
    var = delta.std()
    if var < 1e-12*max(1,abs(dot)): # converged
       perturb...
    # Try step sizes: lr, lr*0.7, lr*0.4 maybe stop at first with M <= currentM*(1+1e-7)?
    currentM = bmax? exact current from b.
    success = False
    for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]: # but if too many conv costly; choose two
       trial_loga = loga - lr*factor*delta
       a_tr = softmax(trial_loga)
       M_tr = conv_max(a_tr)
       if M_tr <= currentM * (1 + 1e-8) or M_tr < bestM:
          loga=trial_loga; a=a_tr; lr = min(5, lr*1.05) maybe; success=True; break
    if not success:
       lr *= 0.5
       # after too many, perturb
       if lr < lr_base*0.05:
          perturb loga with random smooth, set lr=lr_base*0.2
       stall++
    if bestM improved reset stall else stall.
    if stall > threshold: restart from best+random perturbation.
```

Another local move: Since objective homogeneous, transfers preserving sum. For small transfer δ from i to j, change in b_k linear+quadratic:

Convolution exact max:
```
def seq_conv_max(a):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.fft(a, N)
    return np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:L].max()
```

1. `normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s
```

2. `fft_size_for(n)` maybe.

3. `conv_fft(a)`: returns b, A_r,N.
```
def conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Ar*Ar, N)
    b = b[:L] # due small negative numerical, max unaffected; maybe b=np.maximum(b,0)
    return b, Ar, N
```
For conv_max only:
```
def conv_max_only(a):
   a np; N; Ar=rfft; b=irfft(Ar*Ar,N); return b[:2n-1].max()
```

4. `weighted_grad(Ar, N, weights)`:
```
def weighted_grad(Ar, N, weights):
    L = weights.size
    h = np.zeros(N)
    h[:L] = 2.0 * weights
    Hr = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    G = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Ar) * Hr, N)
    return G[:len(weights.shape?)] # need n = (L+1)//2? pass n
```
Pass n. Ensure real.
Optimization: If weights nonzeros only few, maybe direct loops faster? But use FFT.
Could preallocate h? N changes per n. Each call allocate.

5. `make_weights_from_b(b, K=None, temp_rel_to_n=None, temp_abs=None)`:
Choose active top K and temp. Need robust.
```
def make_weights(b, n=None, K=None, temperature=None):
    L=len(b); M=b.max()
    if M <= 1e-300: 
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(b))]=1; return w
    if K is None or K <=1:
       idx=np.argmax(b); w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1; return w
    K=min(K,L)
    if K >= L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       # argpartition for top K. If K close L, maybe use all.
       part = np.argpartition(b, -K) if K < L else np.arange(L)
       idx = part[-K:]
    vals=b[idx]
    # sort descending
    order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    idx=idx[order]; vals=vals[order]
    if temperature is not None and temperature>0:
       T=temperature
    else:
       # default T based on top range
       rng_top = vals[0]-vals[-1]
       # Use effective K: T = rng_top / max(1, log(min(K,1000)))? If rng=0, use M*1e-3
       if not finite or <=1e-15:
           T = max(M*1e-3, 1e-300)
       else:
           T = rng_top / max(1.0, np.log(min(K, 2000)))
           T = max(T, M*1e-6, np.finfo(float).tiny)
    z=(vals-M)/T
    # clip to avoid overflow; z<=0
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0) # If clip, weights beyond -50 tiny; okay.
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       w=np.zeros(L); w[idx[0]]=1; return w
    w=np.zeros(L)
    w[idx]=e/s
    return w
```
Clipping at -50 may make top K weights, outside zero. If K includes values > M -50T, they get small but nonzero. If T from range/logK, min = exp(-logK)=1/K for bottom; okay. Clipping -50 not hurt.

```

6. `fourier_basis(n, K, extra=True)`:
```
def make_fourier_basis(n, K):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   cols=[]
   if extra:
      u=i/(max(n-1,1))-0.5
      cols.append(u)
      cols.append(u*u-1/n?)
   # maybe half sin? include sin(pi*i/n) and cos(pi*i/n)? endpoints? 
   for m in range(1,K+1):
      ang=2*np.pi*m*i/n
      cols.append(np.sin(ang))
      cols.append(np.cos(ang))
   B=np.column_stack(cols)
   # center and scale to unit norm
   for j in range(B.shape[1]):
      col=B[:,j]
      col=col-col.mean()
      norm=np.linalg.norm(col)
      if norm>1e-12: B[:,j]=col/norm
   return B
```
If K=30, dim=62. Norm of sin over grid ~√(n/2), so basis values ~1/√n ~0.01 for n=5000. Coeff scale accordingly. Good.
Includes linear u but centered; gradient can adjust nonperiodic trend. However `exp(z)` with linear trend can strongly shift mass; L-BFGS may.

_FOUR_STATE = {'n': None, 'basis': None, 'T': None}
def fourier_fun(x):
   n=_FOUR_STATE['n']; basis=_FOUR_STATE['basis']; T=_FOUR_STATE['T']
   z = basis @ x
   z -= np.max(z)
   a = np.exp(z)
   s=a.sum()

   else: a/=s
   b, Ar, N = conv_fft(a)

   G = weighted_grad(Ar, N, w, n)

   soft = M + T*np.log(np.sum(w? wait weights normalized; soft needs sume not w denom.

12. Full refine `flow_refine(a0, time_limit, seed=0, K_start=80, step_init=0.1, max_step=0.5, step_floor=1e-3, restart_time=60?)`.
Detailed code:
```
def flow_refine(a0, time_limit, seed=42, K_start=80, step_init=0.1, max_step=0.5, step_min=1e-4):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    # ensure positive
    a = a + 1e-12/n; a/=a.sum()
    loga=np.log(a)
    # precompute?
    best=a.copy(); bestM=conv_max_only(a); bestScore=2*n*bestM
    step=step_init; K=K_start; Kmax=min(2*n-1, max(200, int(0.06*n)))? maybe 0.1n
    stall=0; nobest=0; t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    # maybe vary K gradually
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        if remaining < 0.2: break
        b, Ar, N = conv_fft(a)
        M = b.max()
        if M < bestM - 1e-18:
            bestM=M; best=a.copy(); bestScore=2*n*M; stall=0
        else:
            # M same; if M < bestM maybe numerical; update if M < bestM
            pass
        # adjust K: increase if stall? K = min(Kmax, int(K_start * (1+restart*0.2)))
        w = weights_top(b, K)
        G = weighted_grad(Ar, N, w, n)
        # direction
        support = a > (a.max()*1e-8)
        if support.sum() == 0: support = np.ones(n,bool)
        mu = np.median(G[support]) if np.any(support) else G.mean()
        delta = G - mu
        scale = np.max(np.abs(delta))
        if scale <= 0 or not finite:
            stall += 100; perturb
        else:
            dir = delta/scale
            # We can scale dir by a support? maybe
            # Try candidate
            accepted=False
            for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25]:
                try_step = step*factor
                loga_t = loga - try_step*dir
                # center loga maybe to prevent overflow
                loga_t -= np.max(loga_t)
                a_t=np.exp(loga_t); s=a_t.sum()
                if s<=0 or not finite: continue
                a_t/=s
                M_t=conv_max_only(a_t)
                if M_t <= M*(1+1e-11) or M_t < bestM:
                    loga=loga_t; a=a_t; accepted=True
                    if factor==1.0:
                        step=min(max_step, step*1.05)
                    else:
                        step=max(step_min, step*0.85)
                    if M_t < bestM: best...
                    break
            if not accepted:
                step=max(step_min, step*0.5)
                stall += 1
        if M < bestM: stall=0 else stall+=? (we set above)
        if stall > 200 or (bestM - M) > ...? 
            # perturb around best
            strength = 0.005 + 0.002*min(stall/500,5)
            # random smooth noise in log or a
            noise = random_smooth(n, rng, strength)
            a_p = a* (1+noise)
            a_p = np.maximum(a_p,0); normalize
            # optionally better start at best not current
            if rng.rand()<0.5:
               a_p = best*(1+noise); normalize
            loga=np.log(a_p); step=step_init*0.5; K=min(Kmax, int(K*1.3)+10); stall=0
    return best, bestScore
```

   n=a.size
   G=np.zeros(n)
   active=np.nonzero(weights)[0]

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def _norm(a, positive_floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
       return np.array([1.0])
    if positive_floor:
       a = np.maximum(a, positive_floor)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    return a/s
```
Use positive floor for log: `a = _norm(a0); eps=1e-12/max(n,1); a = np.maximum(a, eps); a=a/a.sum()`.

`conv_fft`:
```
def _conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    Ar=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Ar*Ar, N)
    b = b[:L]
    # clip tiny negatives?
    # b = np.maximum(b,0.0) maybe affects dot? But max maybe tiny positive. For W, clipping okay? Use maximum to avoid negative weights.
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b, Ar, N
```
Clipping negative numerical can make sum >1? just for max/W, fine. For gradient using weights from b, okay. But if b all zero? scores.
`conv_max(a)`: call _conv_fft and return b.max().
`conv_max_and_wsum(a,w)`: b,Ar,N=_conv_fft; return b.max(), np.dot(w,b), b maybe? For line search need b_t to dot; can return b. Use candidate current.

`weights_top_uniform(b, K)`:
```
def _weights_top_uniform(b, K):
    L=b.size
    if K <=1 or K >= L:
       idx=int(np.argmax(b)) if K<=1 else np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if K <=1: w[idx]=1.0
    elif K >= L: w.fill(1.0/L)
    else: w[idx]=1.0/K
    return w
```
If b has many zeros? uniform top K okay.
Could include weights by value to focus near max: maybe optional. Add `weights_top_exp(b,K,temperature_over_range=None)`.
For flow uniform enough. For L-BFGS use fixed temp.

`weighted_grad_hybrid(a, weights, Ar=None, N=None, fft_threshold=250)`:
```
def _weighted_grad(a, weights, Ar=None, N=None, fft_thr=250):
   n=a.size
   idx=np.nonzero(weights)[0]
   if idx.size ==0: return zeros
   if (Ar is not None and idx.size > fft_thr):
       L=weights.size
       h=np.zeros(N)
       h[:L]=2.0*weights
       Hr=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
       G=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Ar)*Hr,N)[:n]
       return G
   G=np.zeros(n)
   for kk in idx:
      wi=weights[kk]
      k=int(kk)
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if lo>hi: continue
      j0=k-lo; j1=k-hi; coeff=2.0*wi
      if j0 == j1:
         G[lo] += coeff*a[j0]
      elif j1 == 0:
         G[lo:hi+1] += coeff*a[j0::-1]
      else:
         G[lo:hi+1] += coeff*a[j0:j1-1:-1]
   return G
```
If idx large and Ar None, direct could be slow but K limited. If Ar not None but idx large, FFT.
Need ensure weights sum maybe 1, G =2w*a. Good.
`np.nonzero(weights)` over dense length L O(L). For uniform top K, K small. We can avoid dense w and pass indices? Could optimize by `weights_top_uniform` also returns indices. But fine L ~10k.

`_weights_fixed_temp(b, T, max_active=4000)`:
```
def _weights_fixed_temp(b, T, max_active=4000):
    L=b.size; M=b.max()
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if M <= 0: w[0]=1; return w,0.0,M
    if T <= 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w[idx]=1; s=1.0; return w,s,M
    thresh = M - max(60.0*T, 1e-14*M)
    act=np.where(b >= thresh)[0]
    if act.size ==0:
       act=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    elif act.size > max_active:
       # top max_active by partition
       part=np.argpartition(b, -max_active)[-max_active:]
       act=part
    z=(b[act]-M)/T
    # clip z since negative
    z=np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       # pick max within act
       amax=int(act[np.argmax(b[act])])
       w[amax]=1.0; s=1.0
    else:
       w[act]=e/s
    return w,s,M
```
If T too large, active all L, max_active maybe 4000 for n=16000 L=31999; exp over 4000 okay. But all b below threshold? threshold M-60T; with T=0.02/n~1e-6, M~1e-4, 60T~6e-5, includes many central peaks maybe >4000. okay.
`soft = M + T*log(s)` (if s from unnormalized). If clipping at -60, sum slightly lower; still approximately. Could not clip? z min -∞ underflow; clip fine.

`fourier_fun` uses `_weights_fixed_temp`.
Need `_score_from_b(b,n)` return 2n max.

`make_fourier_basis`:
```
def _make_fourier_basis(n, K):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols=[]
    if n>1:
       u=(i/(n-1))-0.5
       cols.append(u)
       cols.append(u*u - 1.0/n) # constant part removed? centering later
    # maybe endpoint-cop? include sin(pi * i/n) (DCT-like)
    cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*i/max(n,1)))
    cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*i/max(n,1)))
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2.0*np.pi*m*i/n
       cols.append(np.sin(ang)); cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
       c=B[:,j]
       c = c - c.mean()
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(c)
       if nrm>1e-12: B[:,j]=c/nrm
       else: B[:,j]=0
    return B
```
Columns centered and norm1. Sine pi column at i=0=0, i=n-1=sin(π-π/n) small, not exact endpoint zero. Fine. Could instead include `np.sin(np.pi*(i+0.5)/n)`? Not needed.
Number dim = 2 + 2 + 2K = 2K+4. K=20 ->44.

`_FOUR_STATE`: global dict.
```
_FOUR_STATE = {'n':0, 'basis':None, 'T':1e-6}
def _fourier_fun(x):
    n=int(_FOUR_STATE['n']); B=_FOUR_STATE['basis']; T=float(_FOUR_STATE['T'])
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    try:
       z=B.dot(x)
    except Exception:
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    a=np.exp(zm)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a=a/s
    b,Ar,N=_conv_fft(a)
    M=float(b.max())
    w, summ, M2 = _weights_fixed_temp(b, T, max_active=4000)
    G=_weighted_grad(a, w, Ar, N, fft_thr=200)
    dot=float(np.dot(a,G))
    theta=a*(G-dot)
    grad=B.T @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    soft = M + T * np.log(summ) if summ>0 else M
    if not np.isfinite(soft): soft=1e100
    return float(soft), grad
```
If n=0? no.

`fourier_search`:
```
def fourier_search(n, time_limit, K=22, T_factor=0.02, extra_log=None, seed=123, max_solves=6, maxiter=120):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except Exception:
       return None, None
    B=_make_fourier_basis(n,K)
    starts=[]
    # zero
    starts.append(np.zeros(B.shape[1]))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # extra log
    if extra_log is not None:
       loga=np.log(np.maximum(extra_log,1e-15)); loga-=loga.mean()
       c=B.T @ loga
       c=np.nan_to_num(c)
       starts.append(c)
    for scale in [0.3,0.7,1.2]:
       c=rng.normal(0, scale, B.shape[1])
       # decay high freq maybe: c = c / (1+0.1*np.arange(dim))
       starts.append(c)
    for idx,c0 in enumerate(starts):
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit-0.1: break
       T=T_factor/n
       _FOUR_STATE['n']=n; _FOUR_STATE['basis']=B; _FOUR_STATE['T']=T
       best_x=np.nan_to_num(c0)
       # evaluate initial exact
       a=_soft_x_to_a(best_x,B); sc=...
       update local best
       try:
          res=minimize(_fourier_fun, best_x.copy(), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': int(maxiter*4), 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':8})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
          x2=np.nan_to_num(res.x)
          a=_param_to_a(x2,B); score=...
          update
          # also maybe intermediate? no
    return best_a, best_score
```

```
def flow_refine(a0, time_limit, seed=42, K_start=None, step_init=0.05, max_step=0.3):
    n=len(a0)
    a=_norm(a0)
    eps=1e-12/max(n,1)
    a=np.maximum(a, eps); a=a/a.sum()
    loga=np.log(a)
    if K_start is None:
       K_start=max(40, min(300, int(0.012*n)))
    Kmax=min(2*n-1, max(300, int(0.05*n)))
    best=a.copy(); bestM=conv_max(a); bestScore=2*n*bestM
    step=step_init
    stall=0; no_best_time=0; last_best=time.monotonic()
    t0=...
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    K=K_start
    uniform=True
    it=0
    while elapsed < time_limit:
       if time.monotonic() - last_best > 45:
          # perturb
          strength=0.08 + 0.02*min((elapsed-last_best)/60,5)
          loga = loga + strength*_smooth_noise(n,rng)
          a=_softmax(loga); step=step_init*0.6; K=min(Kmax, int(K*1.25)+10); stall=0; last_best? maybe not.
       K = min(Kmax, int(K_start + (Kmax-K_start)*min(1.0, elapsed/time_limit)))
       b,Ar,N=conv_fft(a)
       M=float(b.max())
       if M < bestM - 1e-18:
           bestM=M; bestScore=2*n*M; best=a.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic()
       # progress by score? maybe if bestScore? track best exact only.
       w=_weights_top_uniform(b,K)
       Wcur=float(np.dot(w,b))
       G=_weighted_grad(a,w,Ar,N,fft_thr=250)
       support = a > (a.max()*1e-8)
       if support.sum() == 0: support=np.ones(n,bool)
       mu = float(np.median(G[support]))
       delta=G-mu
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
       if not finite scale or scale <= 1e-12*M:
          stall += 50
       else:
          dir=delta/scale
          # maybe center dir to zero mean? log update normalization; but to avoid drift, dir -= dir.mean()? Could.
          dir -= dir.mean()
          scale2=max(abs(dir)); if >0 dir/=scale2? If we recenter and renorm, okay.
          # candidate
          accepted=False
          for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
             loga_t=loga - step*factor*dir
             loga_t -= np.max(loga_t)
             a_t=np.exp(loga_t); s=a_t.sum()
             if not finite s: continue
             a_t /= s
             b_t,_,_=conv_fft(a_t)
             M_t=float(b_t.max())
             W_t=float(np.dot(w,b_t))
             # choose if improves surrogate and exact not too bad
             if W_t < Wcur - 1e-14 and M_t < M*1.02:
                 loga=loga_t; a=a_t; accepted=True
                 if factor == 1.0:
                    step=min(max_step, step*1.08)
                 else:
                    step=max(1e-3, step*0.9)
                 if M_t < M: # exact current improved but need compare best
                    pass
                 break
          if not accepted:
             step=max(1e-4, step*0.5)
             stall += 1
       # update best if current a improved (maybe from accepted)
       # The M at start of loop for old a; after accept, need new best maybe M_t. We can recompute bestM with candidate M_t if tracked.
       # Easier after accept store M_t; if M_t < bestM update.
```

`_softmax(loga)`: center and exp, normalize.
```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   z=z-np.max(z)
   a=np.exp(z)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(l)/l
   return a/s
```

`_smooth_noise(n,rng,strength)`: We can generate normalized noise:
```
def _smooth_noise(n, rng, strength=0.1):
    x=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, 13):
       amp1=rng.normal(); amp2=rng.normal(); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       # frequency maybe m (integer) or random mid? Use integer modes 1..12
       f=2π*m/n
       x += amp1*np.sin(f*i) + amp2*np.cos(f*i)
    # add small white? no
    sd=x.std()
    if sd>1e-15:
       x = strength * x / sd
    else:
       x = rng.normal(0,strength,n)
    return x
```
This noise has shape smooth. Could also use linear ramp random. Good.

Need allow small: `1e-13`. Use `1e-12`. Also allow exact improvement: if M_t < M - 1e-14 accept regardless W. So condition:

`interpolate_seq(a, new_n)`:
```
def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    old_n=len(a); if old_n==new_n: return a.copy()
    a=_norm(a)
    old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    y=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return _norm(y)
```
Linear interpolation. For large new_n, perhaps use cubic? Could use `scipy.interpolate`? Not necessary. But linear may create non-smooth if parent discrete; okay. Could use `np.interp` at midpoints.

def sweep_lengths(base_a, max_n, min_n=512, step=256):
    base_n=len(base_a)
    best_seq=base_a; best_score=score_sequence(base_a)
    ns=set()
    if base_n <= max_n: ns.add(base_n)
    for n in range(max(min_n, base_n//2), max_n+1, step): ns.add(n)
    # add multiples? also base*1.2 etc
    for factor in [0.75,0.9,1.1,1.25,1.5,2.0]:
       n=int(base_n*factor); if min_n<=n<=max_n: ns.add(n)
    # round to multiples of 256 maybe
    sorted_ns=sorted(ns)
    for n in sorted_ns:
       if n==base_n: a=base_a; sc=best_score
       else: a=interpolate_seq(base_a,n); sc=score_sequence(a)
       if sc < best_score -1e-12: update
    return best_seq,best_score,best_n,best_M
```

def _params_to_a(x,B):
   z=np.dot(B,x)

   a=np.exp(z)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300 or not finite: return np.ones(B.shape[0])/B.shape[0]
   return a/s
```

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(float(budget_s), 1800.0) - 25.0
    best_seq=None; best_score=float('inf')
    # parent
    parent = None
    try:
       p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
       p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
       p = np.clip(p,0,1000)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size >= 100 and p.sum()>1e-6:
          parent = p/p.sum()
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None:
       best_seq=parent.copy(); best_score=_score_sequence(best_seq)
       print(...)
    else:
       n=5000
       i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
       z=0.6*np.sin(2*np.pi*i/n)+0.3*np.sin(4*np.pi*i/n)
       a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum()
       best_seq=a; best_score=_score_sequence(a)
    best_n=len(best_seq)
    print(...)
    # length sweep
    rem = limit - (time.monotonic()-t_start)
    if rem > 5:
       max_n = min(16000, max(2000, int(best_n*2.0)))
       seq,score,n = sweep_lengths(best_seq, max_n)
       if score < best_score: best_seq=seq; best_score=score; best_n=n
    # flow1
    rem = limit - elapsed
    if rem > 60:
       t_flow1 = min(rem*0.45, 750.0)
       out_seq,out_score=flow_refine(best_seq, t_flow1, seed=7)
       if out_score < best_score: update
    # fourier
    rem=...
    if rem > 60:
       t_four = min(rem*0.30, 450.0)
       # choose n: best_n and maybe larger
       n_list=[best_n]
       for cand in [int(best_n*1.2), int(best_n*1.5), min(16000, best_n+3000)]: if 1500<=cand<=16000 and cand not in n_list: add
       for n in n_list:
           if rem < 30: break
           t_i = min(rem*0.4, 150.0) # maybe
           extra_log = np.log(np.maximum(best_seq,1e-15)); extra_log-=mean
           # If n != len(best_seq), interpolate logs? Use best_seq interpolated to n for start
           start_seq = interpolate_seq(best_seq,n) if n!=len else best_seq
           add_log = np.log(start_seq); subtract mean
           a_f,s_f = fourier_search(n, t_i, K=22, T_factor=0.02, extra_log=add_log, seed=100+n%1000)
           if s_f < best_score: update
    # flow2
    rem=...
    if rem > 40:
       t_flow2 = rem - 10
       out_seq,out_score=flow_refine(best_seq, t_flow2, seed=101)
       if out_score < best_score: update
    # final small length sweep around best_n
    rem=...

    # final sym? try sym and maybe reverse (same) not much; maybe try zeroing smallest 1%? not.
    final_list=[float(v) for v in np.asarray(best_seq)]
    try:
       official=evaluate_sequence(final_list)
       if not finite: official=best_score
    except: official=best_score
    print final
    return final_list
```

Pseudo full:
```
def flow_refine(a0, time_limit, seed=42, step_init=0.06, max_step=0.35):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0,float)
    n = a0.size
    if time_limit <=0 or n<2: return a0.copy(), _score_sequence(a0)
    a = _norm(a0)
    eps=1e-12/n
    a = np.maximum(a, eps); a=a/a.sum()
    loga = np.log(a)
    K_start = max(50, min(350, int(0.015*n)))
    K_max = min(2*n-1, max(600, int(0.06*n)))
    best = a.copy()
    b0,_,_ = _conv_fft(a)
    bestM = float(b0.max())
    bestScore = 2.0*n*bestM
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step = step_init
    stall = 0
    last_best = t0
    restart = 0
    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit - 0.2: break
        # no best perturbation
        if elapsed - last_best > 55.0:
            restart += 1
            strength = min(0.35, 0.06 * (1 + 0.25*(restart-1)))
            loga = np.log(best) + strength * _smooth_noise(n, rng, 1.0)  # _smooth returns std1*strength? We'll make takes strength
            loga -= np.max(loga)
            a = _softmax(loga)
            step = max(step_init*0.6, step*0.7)
            K_cur = min(K_max, int(K_cur*1.25) + 20) if 'K_cur'...
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0
        K = int(K_start + (K_max-K_start)*min(1.0, elapsed/time_limit))
        K = max(1, min(K, 2*n-1))
        b, Ar, N = _conv_fft(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-18:
            bestM=M; best=a.copy(); bestScore=2*n*M; last_best=time.monotonic(); stall=0
        w = _weights_top_uniform(b, K)
        Wcur = float(np.dot(w,b))
        G = _weighted_grad(a, w, Ar, N, fft_thr=220)
        support = a > (a.max() * 1e-9)
        if support.sum() < 10:
            support = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        mu = float(np.median(G[support]))
        delta = G - mu
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
        if (not np.isfinite(scale)) or scale <= 1e-12 * max(M,1e-15):
            stall += 100
        else:
            dir = delta / scale
            dir -= dir.mean()
            dn = float(np.max(np.abs(dir)))
            if not np.isfinite(dn) or dn <= 1e-15:
                stall += 100
            else:
                dir /= dn
                accepted = False
                candM = M
                for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
                    step_try = step * factor
                    loga_t = loga - step_try * dir
                    # abnormally large values? clip? maybe clip loga_t to max-min? If max-min > 700, small underflow, okay. Could clip to 600 to avoid overflow after center? exp center safe.
                    loga_t -= np.max(loga_t)
                    a_t = np.exp(loga_t)
                    s_t = float(a_t.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(s_t) or s_t <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    a_t = a_t / s_t
                    b_t, _, _ = _conv_fft(a_t)
                    M_t = float(b_t.max())
                    W_t = float(np.dot(w, b_t))
                    improve_exact = M_t < M - 1e-14
                    improve_sur = (W_t < Wcur - 1e-13 * max(1.0, Wcur))
                    if improve_exact or (improve_sur and M_t <= M * 1.001):
                        loga = loga_t
                        a = a_t
                        accepted = True
                        candM = M_t
                        if factor == 1.0:
                            step = min(max_step, step * 1.06)
                        else:
                            step = max(1e-4, step * 0.9)
                        break
                if not accepted:
                    step = max(1e-4, step * 0.55)
                    stall += 1
                if candM < bestM - 1e-18:
                    bestM = candM; best=a.copy(); bestScore=2*n*bestM; last_best=time.monotonic(); stall=0
        if step < 1e-3:
            stall += 20
    # maybe sym try
    return best, bestScore
```

`_smooth_noise`:
```
def _smooth_noise(n, rng, strength=0.1):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    x=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, 12):
       f=2.0*np.pi*m/n
       c1=rng.normal(); c2=rng.normal()
       x += c1*np.sin(f*i) + c2*np.cos(f*i)
    sd=float(x.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        x = (strength / sd) * x
    else:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, strength, n)
    return x
```
Note `np.cos(f*i)` includes constant mode; random smooth. Good.

One issue: When restarting from best, we should evaluate best maybe. If perturb makes worse, flow can recover. Good.

Let's write the code carefully.

```python
"""
Searches for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
2*n*max(full_convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
  1. Load height_sequence_1 if present, otherwise use a smooth default.
  2. Fast multi-length sweep by interpolating the current best sequence.
  3. Full-dimensional local refinement using top-K peak averaging and an
     adaptive multiplicative/exponential step, with hard exact-score tracking.
  4. Low-dimensional Fourier-subspace optimization (L-BFGS with a smooth
     soft-max surrogate) to escape/seed new basins.
  5. Final polish by smoothing, small-tail removal, and nearby-length checks.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_FOUR_STATE = {"n": 0, "basis": None, "T": 1e-6}


def _norm(a):
    """Normalize to non-negative sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _conv_fft(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a by FFT. Returns (b, Ar, N)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64), np.zeros(1, dtype=np.complex128), 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Ar * Ar, N)
    b = b[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b, Ar, N


def _score_sequence(a):
    """Fast internal score: 2*n*max(conv) for normalized nonnegative a."""
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b, _, _ = _conv_fft(a)
    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _weights_top_uniform(b, K):
    """Uniform weights on top K convolution lags."""
    L = b.size
    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if K <= 1:
        idx = int(np.argmax(b)) if L else 0
        w[idx] = 1.0
    elif K >= L:
        w.fill(1.0 / L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    return w


def _weights_fixed_temp(b, T, max_active=12000):
    """Softmax weights over near-peak lags with absolute temperature T."""
    L = b.size
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return w, 1.0, 0.0
    M = float(b.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w, 1.0, M
    if T is None or T <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, 1.0, M

    thresh = M - max(60.0 * T, 1e-14 * M)
    act = np.where(b >= thresh)[0]
    if act.size == 0:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
    elif act.size > max_active:
        act = np.argpartition(b, -int(max_active))[-int(max_active):]

    z = (b[act] - M) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    summ = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(summ) or summ <= 1e-300:
        amax = int(act[int(np.argmax(b[act]))])
        w[amax] = 1.0
        summ = 1.0
    else:
        w[act] = e / summ
    return w, summ, M


def _weighted_grad(a, weights, Ar=None, N=None, fft_thr=220):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights[k] * conv(a,a)[k] with respect to a.
    Uses direct sparse loops for small active sets, FFT otherwise.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if weights is None or n == 0:
        return G

    idx = np.nonzero(weights)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return G

    if Ar is not None and N is not None and idx.size > fft_thr:
        L = weights.size
        h = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        h[:L] = 2.0 * weights
        Hr = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
        Gt = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Ar) * Hr, N)
        return Gt[:n]

    for kk in idx:
        wi = float(weights[kk])
        if wi == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        j0 = k - lo
        j1 = k - hi
        coeff = 2.0 * wi
        if j0 == j1:
            G[lo] += coeff * a[j0]
        elif j1 == 0:
            G[lo:hi + 1] += coeff * a[j0::-1]
        else:
            G[lo:hi + 1] += coeff * a[j0:j1 - 1:-1]
    return G


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return a / s


def _smooth_noise(n, rng, strength=0.1):
    """Low-frequency smooth zero-mean noise with standard deviation ~= strength."""
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 12):
        f = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) / max(1, n)
        x += float(rng.normal()) * np.sin(f * i) + float(rng.normal()) * np.cos(f * i)
    sd = float(x.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        x = (strength / sd) * x
    else:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, strength, n)
    return x


def flow_refine(a0, time_limit, seed=42, step_init=0.06, max_step=0.35):
    """
    Full-length local optimizer.
    Maintains an exponential parameterization and minimizes the average of the
    top-K convolution peaks, accepting moves by exact peak improvement or by
    surrogate improvement with controlled exact-peak increase.
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a0.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a0.copy(), _score_sequence(a0)

    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    a = _norm(a0)
    eps = 1e-12 / max(1, n)
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    loga = np.log(a)

    K_start = int(max(50, min(350, 0.015 * n)))
    K_max = int(min(2 * n - 1, max(600, 0.06 * n)))

    b0, _, _ = _conv_fft(a)
    bestM = float(b0.max())
    best = a.copy()
    last_best = t0

    step = float(step_init)
    stall = 0
    restart_count = 0

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit - 0.25:
            break

        if elapsed - last_best > 55.0:
            restart_count += 1
            strength = min(0.35, 0.05 * (1.0 + 0.3 * min(restart_count, 12)))
            loga = np.log(best) + strength * _smooth_noise(n, rng, 1.0)
            a = _softmax(loga)
            step = max(float(step_init) * 0.6, step * 0.7)
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0
            continue

        K = int(K_start + (K_max - K_start) * min(1.0, elapsed / max(time_limit, 1e-9)))
        K = int(max(1, min(K, 2 * n - 1)))

        b, Ar, N = _conv_fft(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-18:
            bestM = M
            best = a.copy()
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0

        w = _weights_top_uniform(b, K)
        Wcur = float(np.dot(w, b))

        G = _weighted_grad(a, w, Ar, N, fft_thr=220)

        support = a > (float(a.max()) * 1e-9)
        if support.sum() < 8:
            support = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        if support.any():
            mu = float(np.median(G[support]))
        else:
            mu = 0.0

        delta = G - mu
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
        if (not np.isfinite(scale)) or scale <= 1e-12 * max(M, 1e-15):
            stall += 100
            continue

        d = delta / scale
        d -= float(np.mean(d))
        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if (not np.isfinite(dmax)) or dmax <= 1e-15:
            stall += 100
            continue
        d = d / dmax

        accepted = False
        candM = M

        for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
            try_step = step * factor
            loga_t = loga - try_step * d
            loga_t = loga_t - float(np.max(loga_t))
            a_t = np.exp(loga_t)
            s_t = float(a_t.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s_t) or s_t <= 1e-300:
                continue
            a_t = a_t / s_t

            b_t, _, _ = _conv_fft(a_t)
            M_t = float(b_t.max())
            W_t = float(np.dot(w, b_t))

            exact_impr = M_t < M - 1e-14
            surr_impr = W_t < Wcur - 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(Wcur))

            if exact_impr or (surr_impr and M_t <= M * 1.02):
                loga = loga_t
                a = a_t
                accepted = True
                candM = M_t
                if factor == 1.0:
                    step = min(max_step, step * 1.06)
                else:
                    step = max(1e-4, step * 0.90)
                break

        if not accepted:
            step = max(1e-4, step * 0.55)
            stall += 1

        if candM < bestM - 1e-18:
            bestM = candM
            best = a.copy()
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0

        if step < 1e-3:
            stall += 20

    return best, float(2.0 * n * bestM)


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    """Linearly interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    a = _norm(a)
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return _norm(y)


def sweep_lengths(base_a, max_n=15000, min_n=1024, step=512):
    """Evaluate interpolated versions of base_a at several lengths."""
    base_a = _norm(base_a)
    base_n = base_a.size
    if base_n <= 0:
        base_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048
        base_n = 2048

    max_n = int(max(max_n, base_n))
    best = base_a.copy()
    best_score = _score_sequence(best)

    lo = int(max(min_n, base_n // 2))
    if lo > max_n:
        lo = max(1, // max_n)

    ns = set()
    if lo <= base_n <= max_n:
        ns.add(base_n)
    for n in range(lo, max_n + 1, step):
        ns.add(n)
    for delta in (-1024, -512, 0, 512, 1024, 1536, 2048):
        n = base_n + delta
        if lo <= n <= max_n:
            ns.add(n)
    for fac in (0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75):
        n = int(round(base_n * fac))
        if lo <= n <= max_n:
            ns.add(n)

    best_n = base_n
    count = 0
    for n in sorted(ns):
        count += 1
        if n == base_n:
            a = best
            sc = best_score
        else:
            a = interpolate_seq(best, n)
            sc = _score_sequence(a)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = a.copy()
            best_n = n
            print(f"  length {n}: {sc:.6f} (sweep {count})")
    return best, float(best_score), int(best_n)


def make_default(n=5000):
    """A simple positive smooth starting sequence."""
    n = int(max(512, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = (
        0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * i / n)
        + 0.30 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * i / n)
        + 0.20 * (i / n - 0.5)
    )
    a = np.exp(z)
    return a / float(a.sum())


def _make_fourier_basis(n, K):
    """Centered unit-norm basis: a few non-Fourier modes plus low Fourier modes."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    K = int(max(1, K))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []

    u = i / max(1, n - 1) - 0.5
    cols.append(u)
    cols.append(u * u)
    cols.append(np.sin(np.pi * i / max(1, n)))
    cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * i / max(1, n)))

    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))

    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
        c = B[:, j]
        c = c - float(c.mean())
        nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(c))
        if nrm > 1e-12:
            B[:, j] = c / nrm
        else:
            B[:, j] = 0.0
    return B


def _params_to_a(x, B):
    """Map coefficient vector x to normalized positive sequence exp(Bx)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = B.shape[0]
    z = B.dot(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z)) if z.size else z
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def _fourier_fun(x):
    """Soft-max objective and gradient for Fourier coefficients."""
    n = int(_FOUR_STATE["n"])
    B = _FOUR_STATE["basis"]
    T = float(_FOUR_STATE["T"])
    if B is None or n <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.shape[0] != B.shape[1]:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(x.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        z = B.dot(x)
    except Exception:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(x.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    b, Ar, N = _conv_fft(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    w, summ, _ = _weights_fixed_temp(b, T, max_active=12000)
    G = _weighted_grad(a, w, Ar, N, fft_thr=220)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, G))
    theta = a * (G - dot)
    grad = B.T.dot(theta)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    soft = M + T * float(np.log(summ)) if summ > 0.0 and np.isfinite(summ) else M
    if not np.isfinite(soft):
        soft = 1e100
    return float(soft), grad


def fourier_search(n, time_limit, K=20, T_factor=0.02, extra_log=None, seed=123, max_solves=4):
    """
    Low-dimensional Fourier subspace optimization.
    Uses a smooth soft-max surrogate for L-BFGS, then evaluates exact score.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    n = int(max(512, n))
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    if time_limit < 5.0:
        return None, None

    B = _make_fourier_basis(n, int(K))
    dim = B.shape[1]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64))

    if extra_log is not None:
        loga = np.asarray(extra_log, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if loga.size == n:
            loga = np.maximum(loga, -30.0)
            loga = loga - float(np.mean(loga))
            c = B.T.dot(loga)
            c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            starts.append(np.clip(c, -3.5, 3.5))

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    zsin = 0.75 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * i / n) + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * i / n)
    csin = B.T.dot(zsin)
    csin = np.nan_to_num(csin, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    starts.append(np.clip(csin, -3.5, 3.5))

    for amp in (0.55, 0.95, 1.45):
        c = rng.normal(0.0, amp, dim)
        c = c / (1.0 + 0.18 * np.arange(dim, dtype=np.float64))
        c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        starts.append(np.clip(c, -3.5, 3.5))

    starts = starts[: int(max(1, max_solves))]

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for si, c0 in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 0.2:
            break

        c0 = np.nan_to_num(c0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        _FOUR_STATE["n"] = n
        _FOUR_STATE["basis"] = B
        _FOUR_STATE["T"] = float(max(1e-9, T_factor / n))

        a_init = _params_to_a(c0, B)
        sc_init = _score_sequence(a_init)
        if sc_init < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_init)
            best_a = a_init.copy()

        if _scipy_minimize is not None:
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _fourier_fun,
                    c0.copy(),
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 90,
                        "ftol": 1e-10,
                        "gtol": 1e-7,
                        "maxls": 8,
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                x2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                x2 = np.nan_to_num(x2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                a2 = _params_to_a(x2, B)
                sc2 = _score_sequence(a2)
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_a = a2.copy()
        else:
            # Fallback: local random search in Fourier space.
            for _ in range(25):
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 0.2:
                    break
                ct = np.clip(c0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, dim), -3.5, 3.5)
                at = _params_to_a(ct, B)
                st = _score_sequence(at)
                if st < best_score:
                    best_score = float(st)
                    best_a = at.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def final_polish(a0, time_limit=10.0):
    """Cheap deterministic/tail polish after main optimization."""
    a = _norm(a0)
    if a.size < 8:
        return a, _score_sequence(a)

    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_sequence(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    def _try(y):
        nonlocal_best_holder = None
        y = _norm(np.maximum(np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64), 0.0))
        sc = _score_sequence(y)
        return y, sc

    # Rolling smoothing.
    for win in (3, 5, 9, 15):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_limit - 0.1):
            break
        kern = np.ones(int(win), dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
        y = np.convolve(best, kern, mode="same")
        y, sc = _try(y)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = y.copy()

        y = 0.55 * best + 0.45 * y
        y, sc = _try(y)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = y.copy()

    # Remove a very small tail of tiny coefficients.
    for frac in (0.001, 0.003, 0.008, 0.02):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_limit - 0.1):
            break
        y = best.copy()
        if y.size > 50:
            thr = float(np.quantile(y, frac))
            y[y < thr] = 0.0
        y, sc = _try(y)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = y.copy()

    # Mild endpoint suppression.
    for mult in (0.5, 0.1, 0.01):
        y = best.copy()
        y[0] *= mult
        y[-1] *= mult
        y, sc = _try(y)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = y.copy()
            break

    return best, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """Main search entry point. Returns a list of non-negative heights."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        hard_limit = min(float(budget_s), 1800.0) - 28.0
    except Exception:
        hard_limit = 1772.0
    if hard_limit <= 5.0:
        hard_limit = 30.0

    MAX_N = 15000

    # ---- Load parent / default start ----
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 64 and p.sum() > 1e-8:
            if p.size > MAX_N:
                p = interpolate_seq(p, MAX_N)
            parent = p / float(p.sum())
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = _score_sequence(best_seq)
        print(f"parent n={len(best_seq)} score={best_score:.6f}")
    else:
        best_seq = make_default(5000)
        best_score = _score_sequence(best_seq)
        print(f"default n={len(best_seq)} score={best_score:.6f}")

    best_seq = _norm(best_seq)
    best_n = len(best_seq)

    # ---- Length sweep ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 15.0:
        try:
            seq2, sc2, n2 = sweep_lengths(best_seq, max_n=MAX_N, min_n=1024, step=512)
            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_seq = seq2
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_n = int(n2)
                print(f"after sweep n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("sweep error:", repr(e))

    # ---- Main full-space refine ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 60.0:
        t_flow1 = min(rem * 0.45, 700.0)
        if t_flow1 > 25.0:
            try:
                seq2, sc2 = flow_refine(best_seq, t_flow1, seed=17, step_init=0.06, max_step=0.35)
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_seq = seq2
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_n = len(best_seq)
                    print(f"after flow1 n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as e:
                print("flow1 error:", repr(e))

    # ---- Fourier-subspace search ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 60.0:
        t_four_total = min(rem * 0.30, 420.0)
        if t_four_total > 25.0:
            n_list = []
            candidates = [
                best_n,
                int(round(best_n * 1.15)),
                int(round(best_n * 1.35)),
                8000,
                12000,
            ]
            for n in candidates:
                if 1500 <= n <= MAX_N and n not in n_list:
                    n_list.append(int(n))
            n_list = n_list[:3]

            for ii, n in enumerate(n_list):
                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
                rem_now = hard_limit - elapsed
                if rem_now < 35.0:
                    break
                t_i = min(t_four_total / max(1, len(n_list) - ii), rem_now * 0.45, 170.0)
                if t_i < 12.0:
                    break
                try:
                    start_n = n
                    start_seq = interpolate_seq(best_seq, start_n)
                    loga = np.clip(np.log(np.maximum(start_seq, 1e-15)), -30.0, 30.0)
                    loga = loga - float(np.mean(loga))
                    a_f, sc_f = fourier_search(
                        n,
                        t_i,
                        K=20,
                        T_factor=0.02,
                        extra_log=loga,
                        seed=400 + int(n) % 997,
                        max_solves=4,
                    )
                    if a_f is None or sc_f is None or not np.isfinite(sc_f):
                        continue

                    if sc_f < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_seq = _norm(a_f)
                        best_score = float(sc_f)
                        best_n = len(best_seq)
                        print(f"fourier n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
                    elif sc_f < best_score + 3e-4:
                        rem_now2 = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
                        if rem_now2 > 100.0:
                            tq = min(50.0, (rem_now2 - 40.0) * 0.18)
                            if tq > 12.0:
                                a_q, sc_q = flow_refine(_norm(a_f), tq, seed=700 + int(n) % 997)
                                if sc_q < best_score - 1e-12:
                                    best_seq = a_q
                                    best_score = float(sc_q)
                                    best_n = len(best_seq)
                                    print(f"fourier+quick n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
                except Exception as e:
                    print("fourier error:", repr(e))

    # ---- Second full-space refine on current best ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 45.0:
        t_flow2 = rem - 10.0
        if t_flow2 > 25.0:
            try:
                seq2, sc2 = flow_refine(best_seq, t_flow2, seed=2024, step_init=0.05, max_step=0.32)
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_seq = seq2
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_n = len(best_seq)
                    print(f"after flow2 n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as e:
                print("flow2 error:", repr(e))

    # ---- Final cheap polish ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 6.0:
        try:
            seq3, sc3 = final_polish(best_seq, time_limit=max(2.0, rem - 3.0))
            if sc3 < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_seq = seq3
                best_score = float(sc3)
                best_n = len(best_seq)
                print(f"after polish n={best_n} score={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("polish error:", repr(e))

    # ---- Nearby-length final check ----
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if rem > 6.0:
        for delta in (-512, -256, 256, 512, 1024):
            rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if rem < 2.5:
                break
            n2 = best_n + delta
            if 1000 <= n2 <= MAX_N:
                try:
                    a2 = interpolate_seq(best_seq, n2)
                    sc2 = _score_sequence(a2)
                    if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_seq = a2
                        best_score = float(sc2)
                        best_n = n2
                        print(f"final length {n2}: {best_score:.6f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

    best_seq = _norm(np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    final_list = [float(v) for v in best_seq]

    # Official validation, if available. Use only once on final candidate.
    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        official = float(best_score)

    if not np.isfinite(official):
        fb = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        final_list = [float(v) for v in fb]
        try:
            official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        except Exception:
            official = 2.0

    print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.6f} official={official:.6f} n={len(final_list)} time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")
    return final_list
```
